   2  0000              ; Build NeoGS firmware and one deployable package from the Project 124 root:

   5  0000              ; neogs_code.bin       custom firmware, loaded at logical RAM address $0000

# file opened: main.asm
   1+ 0000              ; =========================================================================
   2+ 0000              ; PROJECT 124 — NeoGS Z80 firmware / command dispatcher
   3+ 0000              ; Based on NeoGS Programmer's Guide v0.1.A and ports.inc v0.3.
   4+ 0000              ;
   5+ 0000              ; MEMORY MAP / BOOT:
   6+ 0000              ; The stock GS105a ROM loads a tiny module at $5B00. That module enables
   7+ 0000              ; RAM-over-ROM and receives this firmware at logical RAM address $0000.
   8+ 0000              ; The firmware occupies $0000-$2FFF; its stack is at $3FFF.
   9+ 0000              ; NeoGS logical $4000-$7FFF is the second half of physical RAM page 1,
  10+ 0000              ; therefore logical $5000 maps to physical linear address $00D000.
  11+ 0000              ; The x86 DMA frame is placed at $5000: [length lo][length hi][x86 bytes].
  12+ 0000              ; The interpreter starts at $5002 and is bounded by the exact file length.
  13+ 0000              ; =========================================================================
  14+ 0000
  15+ 0000                      INCLUDE "../shared/neogs_io.inc"
# file opened: neogs_io.inc
   1++0000              ; =========================================================================
   2++0000              ; NeoGS ports.inc v0.3 — Project 124 used subset
   3++0000              ; Hardware port names/addresses from NedoPC ports.inc v0.3.
   4++0000              ; =========================================================================
   5++0000
   6++0000              ; ZX Spectrum side
   7++0000              GSCOM           EQU #BB             ; write command / read status
   8++0000              GSSTAT          EQU #BB             ; read CBIT (bit 0), DBIT (bit 7)
   9++0000              GSDAT           EQU #B3             ; read/write data byte
  10++0000              GSCTR           EQU #33             ; global control
  11++0000
  12++0000              B_CBIT          EQU 0
  13++0000              M_CBIT          EQU #01
  14++0000              B_DBIT          EQU 7
  15++0000              M_DBIT          EQU #80
  16++0000
  17++0000              C_GRST          EQU #80
  18++0000              C_GNMI          EQU #40
  19++0000              C_GLED          EQU #20
  20++0000
  21++0000              ; NeoGS Z80 local ports
  22++0000              MPAG            EQU #00
  23++0000              MPAGEX          EQU #10             ; independent 16 KiB page for C000-FFFF
  24++0000              ZXCMD           EQU #01
  25++0000              ZXDATRD         EQU #02
  26++0000              ZXDATWR         EQU #03
  27++0000              ZXSTAT          EQU #04
  28++0000              CLRCBIT         EQU #05
  29++0000
  30++0000              GSCFG0          EQU #0F
  31++0000              B_NOROM         EQU 0
  32++0000              M_NOROM         EQU #01
  33++0000              B_RAMRO         EQU 1
  34++0000              M_RAMRO         EQU #02
  35++0000              B_8CHANS        EQU 2
  36++0000              M_8CHANS        EQU #04
  37++0000              B_EXPAG         EQU 3
  38++0000              M_EXPAG         EQU #08
  39++0000              B_CKSEL0        EQU 4
  40++0000              M_CKSEL0        EQU #10
  41++0000              B_CKSEL1        EQU 5
  42++0000              M_CKSEL1        EQU #20
  43++0000              C_10MHZ         EQU #30
  44++0000              C_12MHZ         EQU #10
  45++0000              C_20MHZ         EQU #20
  46++0000              C_24MHZ         EQU #00
  47++0000              B_PAN4CH        EQU 6
  48++0000              M_PAN4CH        EQU #40
  49++0000              B_INV7B         EQU 7
  50++0000              M_INV7B         EQU #80
  51++0000
  52++0000              ; NeoGS DMA registers
  53++0000              DMA_MOD         EQU #1B
  54++0000              DMA_HAD         EQU #1C
  55++0000              DMA_MAD         EQU #1D
  56++0000              DMA_LAD         EQU #1E
  57++0000              DMA_CST         EQU #1F
  58++0000              C_DMA_NONE      EQU 0
  59++0000              C_DMA_ZX        EQU 1
  60++0000              C_DMA_SD        EQU 2
  61++0000              C_DMA_MP3       EQU 3
  62++0000
  63++0000              ; Standard General Sound ROM commands.
  64++0000              GS_CMD_RESET_FLAGS  EQU #00
  65++0000              GS_CMD_GET_TOTAL_RAM EQU #20
  66++0000              GS_CMD_LOAD_MODULE  EQU #30
  67++0000              GS_CMD_RUN_MODULE   EQU #31
  68++0000              GS_CMD_OPEN_STREAM  EQU #D1
  69++0000              GS_CMD_CLOSE_STREAM EQU #D2
  70++0000              GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART EQU #F4
  71++0000              GS_CMD_JUMP_ADDRESS EQU #13
  72++0000              GS_CMD_RECEIVE_BLOCK EQU #14
  73++0000
  74++0000              ; Project 124 private commands carried by GSCOM after custom firmware starts.
  75++0000              CMD_RESET       EQU #01
  76++0000              CMD_LOAD_X86    EQU #02
  77++0000              CMD_RUN_X86     EQU #03
  78++0000              CMD_GET_STATUS  EQU #04
  79++0000              CMD_MEM_TEST    EQU #05
  80++0000              CMD_BIOS_POST   EQU #07
  81++0000              CMD_INT_TEST    EQU #08
  82++0000              CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST EQU #09
  83++0000              CMD_BIOS_VIDEO_TEST EQU #0A
  84++0000              CMD_RUN_X86_REAL EQU #0B

  91++0000              NGS_RESULT_SIGNATURE   EQU #A5
  92++0000

  94++0000              NGS_STATUS_DBIT EQU M_DBIT
  95++0000
  96++0000              ; The x86 task is executed in logical $5002..$7FFF (12 KB window less header).
  97++0000              NGS_X86_DMA_WINDOW_SIZE EQU #3000
  98++0000              NGS_X86_MAX_CODE_SIZE   EQU #2FFE

  16+ 0000
  17+ 0000                      ORG     $0000
  18+ 0000
  19+ 0000              ROM_RETURN_TRAMPOLINE_ADDR EQU $4000
  20+ 0000              X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR        EQU $5000
  21+ 0000              X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR           EQU $5002
  22+ 0000              DMA_WAIT_LIMIT   EQU #FFFF
  23+ 0000              DMA_WAIT_OUTER   EQU 8
  24+ 0000              IPC_WAIT_LIMIT   EQU #FFFF
  25+ 0000
  26+ 0000              ; Firmware entry vector
  27+ 0000 C3 00 01             jp      start_neoprogram
  28+ 0003 00 00 00...          BLOCK   53, 0
  29+ 0038 FB                   ei
  30+ 0039 C9                   ret
  31+ 003A 00 00 00...          BLOCK   198, 0
  32+ 0100
  33+ 0100              start_neoprogram:
  34+ 0100 F3                   di
  35+ 0101 ED 56                im      1
  36+ 0103 31 FF 3F             ld      sp, $3FFF
  37+ 0106
  38+ 0106                      ; Set Z80 to the documented maximum guaranteed clock: 20 MHz.
  39+ 0106                      ; Do not select 24 MHz: the guide explicitly says it is not guaranteed.
  40+ 0106 DB 0F                in      a, (GSCFG0)
  41+ 0108 E6 CF                and     #CF
  42+ 010A F6 20                or      C_20MHZ
  43+ 010C D3 0F                out     (GSCFG0), a
  44+ 010E
  45+ 010E AF                   xor     a
  46+ 010F 32 7B 03             ld      (neo_dma_error), a
  47+ 0112 32 7C 03             ld      (saved_z80_ax), a
  48+ 0115 32 7D 03             ld      (saved_z80_ax+1), a
  49+ 0118 32 7E 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), a
  50+ 011B 32 7F 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax+1), a
  51+ 011E 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
  52+ 0121 32 79 03             ld      (neo_command), a
  53+ 0124 32 7A 03             ld      (neo_tx_byte), a
  54+ 0127
  55+ 0127                      ; Copy the ROM-return trampoline into unoverlaid RAM at $4000.
  56+ 0127 21 80 03             ld      hl, rom_return_trampoline_code
  57+ 012A 11 00 40             ld      de, ROM_RETURN_TRAMPOLINE_ADDR
  58+ 012D 01 09 00             ld      bc, rom_return_trampoline_end - rom_return_trampoline_code
  59+ 0130 ED B0                ldir
  60+ 0132
  61+ 0132                      ; Notify the Spectrum only after the new firmware is executing.
  62+ 0132 3E A2                ld      a, NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK
  63+ 0134 CD 4F 03             call    neo_send_data_byte
  64+ 0137
  65+ 0137              main_loop:
  66+ 0137                      ; A command exists only while CBIT (ZXSTAT bit 0) is set.
  67+ 0137 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
  68+ 0139 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
  69+ 013B 28 FA                jr      z, main_loop
  70+ 013D
  71+ 013D                      ; Read the command byte from ZXCMD. Reading it does NOT clear CBIT.
  72+ 013D DB 01                in      a, (ZXCMD)
  73+ 013F 32 79 03             ld      (neo_command), a
  74+ 0142
  75+ 0142                      ; Standard GS #F4 cold restart also returns this custom firmware to ROM.
  76+ 0142 FE F4                cp      GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
  77+ 0144 CA 7F 01             jp      z, command_return_to_gsrom
  78+ 0147 FE 01                cp      CMD_RESET
  79+ 0149 CA 82 01             jp      z, command_reset
  80+ 014C FE 02                cp      CMD_LOAD_X86
  81+ 014E CA 9A 01             jp      z, command_load_x86
  82+ 0151 FE 03                cp      CMD_RUN_X86
  83+ 0153 CA E0 01             jp      z, command_run_x86
  84+ 0156 FE 04                cp      CMD_GET_STATUS
  85+ 0158 CA 25 03             jp      z, command_get_status
  86+ 015B FE 05                cp      CMD_MEM_TEST
  87+ 015D CA EF 02             jp      z, command_mem_test
  88+ 0160 FE 07                cp      CMD_BIOS_POST
  89+ 0162 CA F8 02             jp      z, command_bios_post
  90+ 0165 FE 08                cp      CMD_INT_TEST
  91+ 0167 CA 01 03             jp      z, command_int_test
  92+ 016A FE 09                cp      CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST
  93+ 016C CA 0A 03             jp      z, command_int_guest_test
  94+ 016F FE 0A                cp      CMD_BIOS_VIDEO_TEST
  95+ 0171 CA 13 03             jp      z, command_bios_video_test
  96+ 0174 FE 0B                cp      CMD_RUN_X86_REAL
  97+ 0176 CA 1C 03             jp      z, command_run_x86_real
  98+ 0179
  99+ 0179                      ; Unknown command: release CBIT so the host cannot deadlock.
 100+ 0179 AF                   xor     a
 101+ 017A D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 102+ 017C C3 37 01             jp      main_loop
 103+ 017F
 104+ 017F              ; A #F4 command is also the standard GS105a cold-restart command.
 105+ 017F              ; If our firmware is running, reveal the stock ROM again and jump to its
 106+ 017F              ; reset vector. Leave CBIT asserted: the ROM's cold-start path clears it when
 107+ 017F              ; the standard firmware is ready, so the host's long wait is a real handshake.
 108+ 017F              command_return_to_gsrom:
 109+ 017F C3 00 40             jp      ROM_RETURN_TRAMPOLINE_ADDR
 110+ 0182
 111+ 0182              command_reset:
 112+ 0182 AF                   xor     a
 113+ 0183 32 7B 03             ld      (neo_dma_error), a
 114+ 0186 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 115+ 0189 32 7C 03             ld      (saved_z80_ax), a
 116+ 018C 32 7D 03             ld      (saved_z80_ax+1), a
 117+ 018F 32 7E 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), a
 118+ 0192 32 7F 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax+1), a
 119+ 0195 D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 120+ 0197 C3 37 01             jp      main_loop
 121+ 019A
 122+ 019A              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 123+ 019A              ; CMD_LOAD_X86
 124+ 019A              ; 1) Select ZX DMA module and set physical target $00D000.
 125+ 019A              ; 2) Enable DMA by setting DMA_CST bit 7.
 126+ 019A              ; 3) Clear CBIT: this is the READY indication to the host.
 127+ 019A              ; 4) Wait for host's end marker (#FF written to GSCOM, setting CBIT).
 128+ 019A              ; 5) Disable DMA, then clear CBIT to acknowledge completion.
 129+ 019A              ;
 130+ 019A              ; Host uses OUT/LDIR writes into $0000-$3FFF. No dummy write is performed.
 131+ 019A              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 132+ 019A              command_load_x86:
 133+ 019A 3E 01                ld      a, C_DMA_ZX
 134+ 019C D3 1B                out     (DMA_MOD), a
 135+ 019E
 136+ 019E AF                   xor     a
 137+ 019F D3 1C                out     (DMA_HAD), a            ; physical address bits 21:16 = 0
 138+ 01A1 3E D0                ld      a, #D0
 139+ 01A3 D3 1D                out     (DMA_MAD), a            ; physical address bits 15:8 = #D0
 140+ 01A5 AF                   xor     a
 141+ 01A6 D3 1E                out     (DMA_LAD), a            ; physical address bits 7:0 = 0
 142+ 01A8
 143+ 01A8 3E 80                ld      a, #80
 144+ 01AA D3 1F                out     (DMA_CST), a            ; DMA enable = bit 7
 145+ 01AC
 146+ 01AC AF                   xor     a
 147+ 01AD D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a            ; host may now start LDIR
 148+ 01AF
 149+ 01AF 16 08                ld      d, DMA_WAIT_OUTER
 150+ 01B1              .dma_wait_outer:
 151+ 01B1 01 FF FF             ld      bc, DMA_WAIT_LIMIT
 152+ 01B4              .dma_wait_host_end:
 153+ 01B4 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 154+ 01B6 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
 155+ 01B8 20 1A                jr      nz, .dma_host_end
 156+ 01BA 0B                   dec     bc
 157+ 01BB 78                   ld      a, b
 158+ 01BC B1                   or      c
 159+ 01BD 20 F5                jr      nz, .dma_wait_host_end
 160+ 01BF 15                   dec     d
 161+ 01C0 20 EF                jr      nz, .dma_wait_outer
 162+ 01C2
 163+ 01C2                      ; Timeout: close DMA and release the host command line.
 164+ 01C2 AF                   xor     a
 165+ 01C3 D3 1F                out     (DMA_CST), a
 166+ 01C5 D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 167+ 01C7 3E 01                ld      a, 1
 168+ 01C9 32 7B 03             ld      (neo_dma_error), a
 169+ 01CC 3E 03                ld      a, 3
 170+ 01CE 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 171+ 01D1 C3 37 01             jp      main_loop
 172+ 01D4
 173+ 01D4              .dma_host_end:
 174+ 01D4 AF                   xor     a
 175+ 01D5 D3 1F                out     (DMA_CST), a            ; disable DMA before leaving handler
 176+ 01D7 D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a            ; acknowledge host's #FF marker
 177+ 01D9 AF                   xor     a
 178+ 01DA 32 7B 03             ld      (neo_dma_error), a
 179+ 01DD C3 37 01             jp      main_loop
 180+ 01E0
 181+ 01E0              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 182+ 01E0              ; CMD_RUN_X86 — execute the length-framed payload beginning at $5002.
 183+ 01E0              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 184+ 01E0              command_run_x86:
 185+ 01E0 CD E9 01             call    execute_neogs_x86
 186+ 01E3 AF                   xor     a
 187+ 01E4 D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 188+ 01E6 C3 37 01             jp      main_loop
 189+ 01E9
 190+ 01E9              execute_neogs_x86:
 191+ 01E9 3A 7B 03             ld      a, (neo_dma_error)
 192+ 01EC B7                   or      a
 193+ 01ED 28 0C                jr      z, .dma_ok
 194+ 01EF 21 FF FF             ld      hl, $FFFF
 195+ 01F2 22 7E 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 196+ 01F5 3E 03                ld      a, 3
 197+ 01F7 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 198+ 01FA C9                   ret
 199+ 01FB
 200+ 01FB              .dma_ok:
 201+ 01FB                      ; DMA frame begins with a little-endian x86 byte length at $5000.
 202+ 01FB 2A 00 50             ld      hl, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
 203+ 01FE 7C                   ld      a, h
 204+ 01FF B5                   or      l
 205+ 0200 CA 35 02             jp      z, .invalid_x86_length
 206+ 0203
 207+ 0203                      ; Maximum code length is $2FFE: $5002 + length must not exceed $8000.
 208+ 0203 7C                   ld      a, h
 209+ 0204 FE 2F                cp      #2F
 210+ 0206 38 09                jr      c, .x86_length_valid
 211+ 0208 C2 35 02             jp      nz, .invalid_x86_length
 212+ 020B 7D                   ld      a, l
 213+ 020C FE FF                cp      #FF
 214+ 020E D2 35 02             jp      nc, .invalid_x86_length
 215+ 0211
 216+ 0211              .x86_length_valid:
 217+ 0211 11 02 50             ld      de, X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR
 218+ 0214 19                   add     hl, de
 219+ 0215 22 02 0D             ld      (x86_program_end), hl
 220+ 0218
 221+ 0218 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
 222+ 021B 22 7C 03             ld      (saved_z80_ax), hl
 223+ 021E 21 FF FF             ld      hl, #FFFF
 224+ 0221 22 00 0D             ld      (x86_steps_remaining), hl
 225+ 0224 AF                   xor     a
 226+ 0225 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 227+ 0228
 228+ 0228 01 02 50             ld      bc, X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR
 229+ 022B CD 94 09             call    x86_execute_loop
 230+ 022E
 231+ 022E 2A 7C 03             ld      hl, (saved_z80_ax)
 232+ 0231 22 7E 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 233+ 0234 C9                   ret
 234+ 0235
 235+ 0235              .invalid_x86_length:
 236+ 0235 21 FF FF             ld      hl, #FFFF
 237+ 0238 22 7C 03             ld      (saved_z80_ax), hl
 238+ 023B 22 7E 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 239+ 023E 3E 04                ld      a, 4
 240+ 0240 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 241+ 0243 C9                   ret
 242+ 0244
 243+ 0244              ; Execute the loaded DMA task as bounded guest code at 0000:5002.
 244+ 0244              ; The DMA frame is copied into the x86 physical-memory backend before execution.
 245+ 0244              execute_neogs_x86_real:
 246+ 0244                      ; Match the legacy loader's DMA error and length validation.
 247+ 0244 3A 7B 03             ld      a, (neo_dma_error)
 248+ 0247 B7                   or      a
 249+ 0248 28 0D                jr      z, .real_dma_ok
 250+ 024A 21 FF FF             ld      hl, #FFFF
 251+ 024D 22 7E 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 252+ 0250 3E 03                ld      a, 3
 253+ 0252 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 254+ 0255 37                   scf
 255+ 0256 C9                   ret
 256+ 0257
 257+ 0257              .real_dma_ok:
 258+ 0257 2A 00 50             ld      hl, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
 259+ 025A 7C                   ld      a, h
 260+ 025B B5                   or      l
 261+ 025C CA D5 02             jp      z, .real_invalid_length
 262+ 025F 7C                   ld      a, h
 263+ 0260 FE 2F                cp      #2F
 264+ 0262 38 09                jr      c, .real_length_valid
 265+ 0264 C2 D5 02             jp      nz, .real_invalid_length
 266+ 0267 7D                   ld      a, l
 267+ 0268 FE FF                cp      #FF
 268+ 026A D2 D5 02             jp      nc, .real_invalid_length
 269+ 026D
 270+ 026D              .real_length_valid:
 271+ 026D 11 02 50             ld      de, X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR
 272+ 0270 19                   add     hl, de
 273+ 0271 22 C6 16             ld      (x86_real_task_end), hl
 274+ 0274
 275+ 0274 CD 89 03             call    x86_mem_init
 276+ 0277 21 02 50             ld      hl, X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR
 277+ 027A 11 02 50             ld      de, #5002
 278+ 027D ED 4B 00 50          ld      bc, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
 279+ 0281 CD 6F 10             call    x86_real_copy_image
 280+ 0284 DA E2 02             jp      c, .real_copy_failed
 281+ 0287
 282+ 0287                      ; Reset the guest register file to a deterministic real-mode entry.
 283+ 0287 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
 284+ 028A 22 6E 09             ld      (x86_reg_ax), hl
 285+ 028D 22 74 09             ld      (x86_reg_bx), hl
 286+ 0290 22 70 09             ld      (x86_reg_cx), hl
 287+ 0293 22 72 09             ld      (x86_reg_dx), hl
 288+ 0296 22 78 09             ld      (x86_reg_bp), hl
 289+ 0299 22 7A 09             ld      (x86_reg_si), hl
 290+ 029C 22 7C 09             ld      (x86_reg_di), hl
 291+ 029F 22 7E 09             ld      (x86_reg_es), hl
 292+ 02A2 22 84 09             ld      (x86_reg_ds), hl
 293+ 02A5 22 80 09             ld      (x86_reg_cs), hl
 294+ 02A8 21 02 50             ld      hl, #5002
 295+ 02AB 22 86 09             ld      (x86_reg_ip), hl
 296+ 02AE 21 00 70             ld      hl, #7000
 297+ 02B1 22 82 09             ld      (x86_reg_ss), hl
 298+ 02B4 21 00 01             ld      hl, #0100
 299+ 02B7 22 76 09             ld      (x86_reg_sp), hl
 300+ 02BA 21 02 02             ld      hl, #0202
 301+ 02BD 22 88 09             ld      (x86_reg_flags), hl
 302+ 02C0
 303+ 02C0 3E 01                ld      a, 1
 304+ 02C2 32 C5 16             ld      (x86_real_bounds_enable), a
 305+ 02C5 CD FE 10             call    x86_real_execute
 306+ 02C8 F5                   push    af
 307+ 02C9 2A 6E 09             ld      hl, (x86_reg_ax)
 308+ 02CC 22 7E 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 309+ 02CF AF                   xor     a
 310+ 02D0 32 C5 16             ld      (x86_real_bounds_enable), a
 311+ 02D3 F1                   pop     af
 312+ 02D4 C9                   ret
 313+ 02D5
 314+ 02D5              .real_invalid_length:
 315+ 02D5 21 FF FF             ld      hl, #FFFF
 316+ 02D8 22 7E 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 317+ 02DB 3E 04                ld      a, 4
 318+ 02DD 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 319+ 02E0 37                   scf
 320+ 02E1 C9                   ret
 321+ 02E2
 322+ 02E2              .real_copy_failed:
 323+ 02E2 21 01 1D             ld      hl, #1D01
 324+ 02E5 22 7E 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 325+ 02E8 3E 04                ld      a, 4
 326+ 02EA 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 327+ 02ED 37                   scf
 328+ 02EE C9                   ret
 329+ 02EF
 330+ 02EF              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 331+ 02EF              ; CMD_MEM_TEST — destructive test of the reserved 1 MiB x86 RAM region.
 332+ 02EF              ; Result is returned through the existing CMD_GET_STATUS frame.
 333+ 02EF              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 334+ 02EF              command_mem_test:
 335+ 02EF CD C8 03             call    x86_mem_test
 336+ 02F2 AF                   xor     a
 337+ 02F3 D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 338+ 02F5 C3 37 01             jp      main_loop
 339+ 02F8
 340+ 02F8              ; BIOS low-memory foundation POST.
 341+ 02F8              command_bios_post:
 342+ 02F8 CD E6 05             call    bios_post
 343+ 02FB AF                   xor     a
 344+ 02FC D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 345+ 02FE C3 37 01             jp      main_loop
 346+ 0301
 347+ 0301              ; Stage 2A: validate 8086 INT/IRET stack-frame semantics.
 348+ 0301              command_int_test:
 349+ 0301 CD B5 0D             call    x86_int_self_test
 350+ 0304 AF                   xor     a
 351+ 0305 D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 352+ 0307 C3 37 01             jp      main_loop
 353+ 030A
 354+ 030A              ; Stage 2B: execute a guest INT handler through CS:IP and return with IRET.
 355+ 030A              command_int_guest_test:
 356+ 030A CD 29 0F             call    x86_real_mode_test
 357+ 030D AF                   xor     a
 358+ 030E D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 359+ 0310 C3 37 01             jp      main_loop

# file opened: x86_memory.asm
   1++0389              ; =========================================================================
   2++0389              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 physical memory backend for NeoGS GS105a
   3++0389              ;
   4++0389              ; x86 physical $00000-$FFFFF maps to NeoGS RAM pages 4..67
   5++0389              ; (NeoGS physical base $010000, page size $4000 / 16 KiB).
   6++0389              ; Window C000-FFFF is switched through MPAGEX. This keeps firmware in
   7++0389              ; window 0 / page 0 and the current DMA frame in window 1 / page 3.
   8++0389              ;
   9++0389              ; x86_mem_addr is a 20-bit little-endian address:
  10++0389              ;   +0 = bits 7:0, +1 = bits 15:8, +2 = bits 19:16 (valid 0..0F).
  11++0389              ;
  12++0389              ; API:
  13++0389              ;   x86_mem_init: enable NOROM + EXPAG, preserving other GSCFG0 bits.
  14++0389              ;   x86_mem_read8:  IN x86_mem_addr; OUT A=value, carry=invalid address.
  15++0389              ;   x86_mem_write8: IN x86_mem_addr and x86_mem_value; carry=invalid address.
  16++0389              ;   x86_mem_test: destructive test of the reserved 1 MiB x86 RAM region.
  17++0389              ;                 status=0, AX=0001 on pass; status=5, AX=phase:page on fail.
  18++0389              ;
  19++0389              ; The test leaves NOROM+EXPAG enabled. Only window 3 is switched by MPAGEX.
  20++0389              ; =========================================================================
  21++0389
  22++0389              X86_MEM_NGSPAGE_BASE EQU 4
  23++0389              X86_MEM_PAGE_COUNT   EQU 64
  24++0389
  25++0389              x86_mem_init:
  26++0389 DB 0F                in      a, (GSCFG0)
  27++038B F6 09                or      M_NOROM | M_EXPAG
  28++038D D3 0F                out     (GSCFG0), a
  29++038F C9                   ret
  30++0390
  31++0390              ; Convert x86_mem_addr into a Z80 pointer in C000-FFFF.
  32++0390              ; OUT: HL=window pointer, carry clear; carry set if address > FFFFF.
  33++0390              x86_mem_map_addr:
  34++0390 3A D8 05             ld      a, (x86_mem_addr+2)
  35++0393 FE 10                cp      #10
  36++0395 30 1E                jr      nc, .bad_address
  37++0397
  38++0397                      ; page = (physical >> 14) + 4
  39++0397                      ; page index = (high_nibble << 2) | (middle_byte >> 6)
  40++0397 87                   add     a, a
  41++0398 87                   add     a, a
  42++0399 47                   ld      b, a
  43++039A 3A D7 05             ld      a, (x86_mem_addr+1)
  44++039D 07                   rlca
  45++039E 07                   rlca
  46++039F E6 03                and     #03
  47++03A1 B0                   or      b
  48++03A2 C6 04                add     a, X86_MEM_NGSPAGE_BASE
  49++03A4
  50++03A4                      ; MPAGEX uses ROL8(page,1); send ROR8(page,1).
  51++03A4 0F                   rrca
  52++03A5 D3 10                out     (MPAGEX), a
  53++03A7
  54++03A7                      ; Window C000-FFFF maps the selected 16 KiB physical page.
  55++03A7 3A D7 05             ld      a, (x86_mem_addr+1)
  56++03AA E6 3F                and     #3F
  57++03AC F6 C0                or      #C0
  58++03AE 67                   ld      h, a
  59++03AF 3A D6 05             ld      a, (x86_mem_addr)
  60++03B2 6F                   ld      l, a
  61++03B3 B7                   or      a
  62++03B4 C9                   ret
  63++03B5
  64++03B5              .bad_address:
  65++03B5 37                   scf
  66++03B6 C9                   ret
  67++03B7
  68++03B7              ; Read one byte. IN x86_mem_addr[0..2]. OUT A=byte, carry on invalid address.
  69++03B7              x86_mem_read8:
  70++03B7 CD 90 03             call    x86_mem_map_addr
  71++03BA D8                   ret     c
  72++03BB 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
  73++03BC B7                   or      a
  74++03BD C9                   ret
  75++03BE
  76++03BE              ; Write one byte. IN x86_mem_addr[0..2], x86_mem_value.
  77++03BE              x86_mem_write8:
  78++03BE CD 90 03             call    x86_mem_map_addr
  79++03C1 D8                   ret     c
  80++03C2 3A D9 05             ld      a, (x86_mem_value)
  81++03C5 77                   ld      (hl), a
  82++03C6 B7                   or      a
  83++03C7 C9                   ret
  84++03C8
  85++03C8              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  86++03C8              ; Destructive physical RAM mapping test.
  87++03C8              ; Every page receives unique values at offsets 0000 and 3FFF. A second pass
  88++03C8              ; verifies all 128 locations, detecting missing pages and page aliases.
  89++03C8              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------

   1++05E6              ; =========================================================================
   2++05E6              ; PROJECT 124 — BIOS foundation / POST, stage 1
   3++05E6              ;
   4++05E6              ; Establishes the guest-visible low-memory data layout for a future 8086
   5++05E6              ; real-mode BIOS. This stage does not implement executable BIOS ROM or INT
   6++05E6              ; services. IVT is initialized to zero; vectors are installed only after the
   7++05E6              ; CPU INT/IRET path and guest stack semantics are implemented.
   8++05E6              ;
   9++05E6              ; Physical memory profile:
  10++05E6              ;   00000-003FF  IVT (256 entries, 4 bytes each)
  11++05E6              ;   00400-004FF  BIOS Data Area (BDA)
  12++05E6              ;   00500-9FFFF  conventional RAM profile (640 KiB)
  13++05E6              ;   A0000-BFFFF  reserved video memory
  14++05E6              ;   C0000-EFFFF  reserved option-ROM/device window
  15++05E6              ;   F0000-FFFFF  reserved P124 BIOS ROM (not read-only yet)
  16++05E6              ;
  17++05E6              ; Entry: bios_post
  18++05E6              ; Success: x86_exit_status=0, saved_x86_ax=#B105
  19++05E6              ; Failure: x86_exit_status=7, saved_x86_ax=#B1xx
  20++05E6              ; No DMA/IPC transport changes.
  21++05E6              ; =========================================================================
  22++05E6
  23++05E6              BIOS_IVT_BASE             EQU #00000
  24++05E6              BIOS_IVT_SIZE             EQU #00400

  30++05E6              BIOS_TEXT_COLUMNS         EQU 80

   1++0832              ; =========================================================================
   2++0832              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 16-bit ModR/M effective-address decoder foundation
   3++0832              ;
   4++0832              ; IN:  A = ModR/M byte, BC = pointer to next instruction byte.
   5++0832              ; OUT: x86_ea_is_reg, x86_ea_reg, x86_ea_offset, x86_ea_segment.
   6++0832              ;      BC advances past disp8/disp16. Carry set on truncated displacement.
   7++0832              ;
   8++0832              ; Register encoding for r/m and reg fields:
   9++0832              ;   0 AX, 1 CX, 2 DX, 3 BX, 4 SP, 5 BP, 6 SI, 7 DI.
  10++0832              ; Memory default segment is DS; forms containing BP use SS.
  11++0832              ; mod=00,r/m=110 is direct [disp16] and uses DS.
  12++0832              ; =========================================================================
  13++0832
  14++0832              x86_decode_modrm16:
  15++0832 32 8A 09             ld      (x86_modrm_byte), a
  16++0835 E6 C0                and     #C0
  17++0837 07                   rlca
  18++0838 07                   rlca
  19++0839 32 8B 09             ld      (x86_modrm_mod), a
  20++083C
  21++083C 3A 8A 09             ld      a, (x86_modrm_byte)
  22++083F E6 38                and     #38
  23++0841 CB 3F                srl     a
  24++0843 CB 3F                srl     a
  25++0845 CB 3F                srl     a
  26++0847 32 8D 09             ld      (x86_modrm_reg), a
  27++084A
  28++084A 3A 8A 09             ld      a, (x86_modrm_byte)
  29++084D E6 07                and     7
  30++084F 32 8C 09             ld      (x86_modrm_rm), a
  31++0852
  32++0852 3A 8B 09             ld      a, (x86_modrm_mod)
  33++0855 FE 03                cp      3
  34++0857 C2 67 08             jp      nz, .memory_operand
  35++085A
  36++085A                      ; Register-direct operand: no displacement or memory access.
  37++085A 3E 01                ld      a, 1
  38++085C 32 8E 09             ld      (x86_ea_is_reg), a
  39++085F 3A 8C 09             ld      a, (x86_modrm_rm)
  40++0862 32 8F 09             ld      (x86_ea_reg), a
  41++0865 B7                   or      a
  42++0866 C9                   ret
  43++0867
  44++0867              .memory_operand:
  45++0867 AF                   xor     a
  46++0868 32 8E 09             ld      (x86_ea_is_reg), a
  47++086B 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
  48++086E 22 90 09             ld      (x86_ea_offset), hl
  49++0871 2A 84 09             ld      hl, (x86_reg_ds)
  50++0874 22 92 09             ld      (x86_ea_segment), hl
  51++0877
  52++0877                      ; Decode base/index pair. Use explicit 8086 addressing combinations.
  53++0877 3A 8B 09             ld      a, (x86_modrm_mod)
  54++087A B7                   or      a
  55++087B C2 90 08             jp      nz, .not_direct
  56++087E 3A 8C 09             ld      a, (x86_modrm_rm)
  57++0881 FE 06                cp      6
  58++0883 C2 90 08             jp      nz, .not_direct
  59++0886
  60++0886                      ; mod=00,r/m=110: [disp16], no base register.
  61++0886 CD 50 09             call    x86_modrm_fetch_disp16
  62++0889 D8                   ret     c
  63++088A 22 90 09             ld      (x86_ea_offset), hl
  64++088D C3 06 09             jp      .apply_displacement
  65++0890
  66++0890              .not_direct:
  67++0890 3A 8C 09             ld      a, (x86_modrm_rm)
  68++0893 FE 00                cp      0
  69++0895 CA B9 08             jp      z, .bx_si
  70++0898 FE 01                cp      1
  71++089A CA C4 08             jp      z, .bx_di

 161++0940              ; Fetch one displacement byte from the bounded instruction stream.
 162++0940              ; IN/OUT: BC advances, A=byte, carry on end-of-program.
 163++0940              x86_modrm_fetch_disp8:

 187++0964                      ; Z80 supports (BC) indirect addressing only through A.
 188++0964 0A                   ld      a, (bc)
 189++0965 6F                   ld      l, a
 190++0966 03                   inc     bc

 192++0968 67                   ld      h, a

# file opened: x86_interpreter.asm
   1++0994              ; =========================================================================
   2++0994              ; PROJECT 124 — minimal x86 interpreter core for NeoGS Z80
   3++0994              ;
   4++0994              ; Supported opcodes:
   5++0994              ;   90       NOP
   6++0994              ;   40       INC AX (8086 legacy one-byte register encoding)
   7++0994              ;   B8 iw    MOV AX, imm16
   8++0994              ;   F4       HLT (successful task termination)
   9++0994              ;
  10++0994              ; Unsupported opcode: status 2. Instruction budget exhausted: status 1.
  11++0994              ; DMA error: status 3. Out-of-range/truncated instruction: status 4.
  12++0994              ; x86_program_end is the exclusive logical address after the payload.
  13++0994              ;
  14++0994              ; Entry: BC = x86 IP in NeoGS Z80 logical address space; AX stored in
  15++0994              ;        saved_z80_ax. Exit: saved_z80_ax and x86_exit_status are updated.
  16++0994              ; =========================================================================
  17++0994
  18++0994              x86_execute_loop:
  19++0994              x86_next_instruction:
  20++0994                      ; Every opcode fetch must remain strictly below the exclusive end.
  21++0994 2A 02 0D             ld      hl, (x86_program_end)
  22++0997 B7                   or      a
  23++0998 ED 42                sbc     hl, bc
  24++099A DA 04 0A             jp      c, x86_ip_out_of_range
  25++099D 7C                   ld      a, h
  26++099E B5                   or      l
  27++099F CA 04 0A             jp      z, x86_ip_out_of_range
  28++09A2
  29++09A2 2A 00 0D             ld      hl, (x86_steps_remaining)
  30++09A5 7C                   ld      a, h
  31++09A6 B5                   or      l
  32++09A7 20 09                jr      nz, .budget_ok
  33++09A9 3E 01                ld      a, 1
  34++09AB 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
  35++09AE 2A 7C 03             ld      hl, (saved_z80_ax)
  36++09B1 C9                   ret
  37++09B2
  38++09B2              .budget_ok:
  39++09B2 2B                   dec     hl
  40++09B3 22 00 0D             ld      (x86_steps_remaining), hl
  41++09B6
  42++09B6                      ; Fetch opcode and advance IP.
  43++09B6 0A                   ld      a, (bc)
  44++09B7 03                   inc     bc
  45++09B8
  46++09B8                      ; Resolve handler from 256-entry WORD table.
  47++09B8 6F                   ld      l, a
  48++09B9 26 00                ld      h, 0
  49++09BB 29                   add     hl, hl
  50++09BC 11 00 0B             ld      de, x86_jump_table
  51++09BF 19                   add     hl, de
  52++09C0 5E                   ld      e, (hl)
  53++09C1 23                   inc     hl
  54++09C2 56                   ld      d, (hl)
  55++09C3
  56++09C3                      ; Restore AX in HL and jump to the selected handler. PUSH/RET is
  57++09C3                      ; the Z80's safe indirect-jump idiom; BC remains the virtual x86 IP.
  58++09C3 2A 7C 03             ld      hl, (saved_z80_ax)
  59++09C6 D5                   push    de
  60++09C7 C9                   ret
  61++09C8
  62++09C8              ; 90 — NOP
  63++09C8              x86_nop:
  64++09C8 22 7C 03             ld      (saved_z80_ax), hl
  65++09CB C3 94 09             jp      x86_next_instruction
  66++09CE
  67++09CE              ; 40 — INC AX
  68++09CE              x86_inc_ax:
  69++09CE 23                   inc     hl
  70++09CF 22 7C 03             ld      (saved_z80_ax), hl
  71++09D2 C3 94 09             jp      x86_next_instruction
  72++09D5
  73++09D5              ; B8 iw — MOV AX, imm16 (little-endian immediate)
  74++09D5              x86_mov_ax_imm16:
  75++09D5                      ; B8 requires two immediate bytes. Reject a truncated instruction.
  76++09D5 2A 02 0D             ld      hl, (x86_program_end)
  77++09D8 B7                   or      a
  78++09D9 ED 42                sbc     hl, bc
  79++09DB 11 02 00             ld      de, 2
  80++09DE B7                   or      a
  81++09DF ED 52                sbc     hl, de
  82++09E1 DA 04 0A             jp      c, x86_ip_out_of_range
  83++09E4
  84++09E4 69                   ld      l, c
  85++09E5 60                   ld      h, b
  86++09E6 5E                   ld      e, (hl)
  87++09E7 23                   inc     hl
  88++09E8 56                   ld      d, (hl)
  89++09E9 23                   inc     hl
  90++09EA 44                   ld      b, h
  91++09EB 4D                   ld      c, l
  92++09EC ED 53 7C 03          ld      (saved_z80_ax), de
  93++09F0 C3 94 09             jp      x86_next_instruction
  94++09F3
  95++09F3              ; F4 — HLT: successful end of task
  96++09F3              x86_hlt:

  98++09F6 AF                   xor     a

 100++09FA C9                   ret

   1++0D05              ; =========================================================================
   2++0D05              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 real-mode interrupt frame engine (Stage 2A)
   3++0D05              ;
   4++0D05              ; Implements the architectural memory/stack part of software INT and IRET.
   5++0D05              ; This module is deliberately separate from the legacy four-opcode fetch
   6++0D05              ; loop: opcode dispatch and execution of guest handler code are Stage 2B.
   7++0D05              ;
   8++0D05              ; x86_int_enter:
   9++0D05              ;   IN: A = interrupt vector number
  10++0D05              ;   Uses x86_reg_{ss,sp,cs,ip,flags}.
  11++0D05              ;   Push order: FLAGS, CS, IP; final SP points to saved IP.
  12++0D05              ;   Loads new IP:CS from IVT[vector], clears IF and TF.
  13++0D05              ;   OUT: carry clear on success, carry set on memory error.
  14++0D05              ;
  15++0D05              ; x86_iret_return:
  16++0D05              ;   Pops IP, CS, FLAGS; restores all three and advances SP by six.
  17++0D05              ;
  18++0D05              ; x86_int_self_test:
  19++0D05              ;   Installs a temporary vector 20h, validates the exact stack frame,
  20++0D05              ;   validates IF/TF clearing, executes IRET, validates restored state,
  21++0D05              ;   and removes the temporary vector.
  22++0D05              ;   Success: status=0, AX=1A01h. Failure: status=8, AX=1Axxh.
  23++0D05              ; =========================================================================
  24++0D05
  25++0D05              X86_INT_IF_TF_CLEAR_MASK EQU #FC
  26++0D05
  27++0D05              x86_int_enter:
  28++0D05 32 22 0F             ld      (x86_int_vector), a
  29++0D08
  30++0D08                      ; IVT entry address = vector * 4, physical segment zero.
  31++0D08 6F                   ld      l, a
  32++0D09 26 00                ld      h, 0
  33++0D0B 29                   add     hl, hl
  34++0D0C 29                   add     hl, hl

  36++0D10 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
  37++0D13 22 DC 05             ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
  38++0D16 CD A1 04             call    x86_mem_read16
  39++0D19 D8                   ret     c
  40++0D1A 22 25 0F             ld      (x86_int_target_ip), hl
  41++0D1D
  42++0D1D 2A DE 05             ld      hl, (x86_mem_offset)
  43++0D20 23                   inc     hl
  44++0D21 23                   inc     hl
  45++0D22 22 DE 05             ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
  46++0D25 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
  47++0D28 22 DC 05             ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl

  49++0D2E D8                   ret     c
  50++0D2F 22 27 0F             ld      (x86_int_target_cs), hl
  51++0D32
  52++0D32                      ; 8086 interrupt entry pushes FLAGS, then CS, then return IP.
  53++0D32 ED 5B 88 09          ld      de, (x86_reg_flags)
  54++0D36 CD 77 0D             call    x86_int_push_word
  55++0D39 D8                   ret     c
  56++0D3A ED 5B 80 09          ld      de, (x86_reg_cs)
  57++0D3E CD 77 0D             call    x86_int_push_word
  58++0D41 D8                   ret     c
  59++0D42 ED 5B 86 09          ld      de, (x86_reg_ip)
  60++0D46 CD 77 0D             call    x86_int_push_word
  61++0D49 D8                   ret     c
  62++0D4A
  63++0D4A                      ; IF (bit 9) and TF (bit 8) are cleared in the live FLAGS register.
  64++0D4A 3A 89 09             ld      a, (x86_reg_flags+1)
  65++0D4D E6 FC                and     X86_INT_IF_TF_CLEAR_MASK
  66++0D4F 32 89 09             ld      (x86_reg_flags+1), a
  67++0D52 2A 25 0F             ld      hl, (x86_int_target_ip)
  68++0D55 22 86 09             ld      (x86_reg_ip), hl
  69++0D58 2A 27 0F             ld      hl, (x86_int_target_cs)
  70++0D5B 22 80 09             ld      (x86_reg_cs), hl
  71++0D5E B7                   or      a
  72++0D5F C9                   ret
  73++0D60
  74++0D60              x86_iret_return:
  75++0D60 CD 97 0D             call    x86_int_pop_word
  76++0D63 D8                   ret     c
  77++0D64 22 86 09             ld      (x86_reg_ip), hl
  78++0D67
  79++0D67 CD 97 0D             call    x86_int_pop_word
  80++0D6A D8                   ret     c
  81++0D6B 22 80 09             ld      (x86_reg_cs), hl
  82++0D6E
  83++0D6E CD 97 0D             call    x86_int_pop_word
  84++0D71 D8                   ret     c
  85++0D72 22 88 09             ld      (x86_reg_flags), hl
  86++0D75 B7                   or      a
  87++0D76 C9                   ret

  89++0D77              ; IN: DE=word. Decrement guest SP by two, then write SS:SP.

   1++0F29              ; =========================================================================
   2++0F29              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 real-mode fetch/execute core, Stage 2B
   3++0F29              ;
   4++0F29              ; This bounded executable slice runs guest instructions through CS:IP and
   5++0F29              ; the physical-memory backend. Supported: NOP, register-direct 8/16-bit
   6++0F29              ; arithmetic and logical subsets, PUSHF, INC/DEC r16, PUSH/POP r16,
   7++0F29              ; MOV r8/r16,imm, MOV r/m8/r/m16 register-memory forms, short Jcc,
   8++0F29              ; LOOP/JCXZ, JMP rel8, HLT, INT imm8, INT3, IRET.
   9++0F29              ; Arithmetic and 8/16-bit logical memory ModR/M operands support segment overrides.
  10++0F29              ; It is intentionally not a general 8086 emulator.
  11++0F29              ;
  12++0F29              ; x86_real_mode_test installs a guest program at 0000:5002 and an interrupt
  13++0F29              ; handler at 0000:5100. The guest loads all eight general registers with
  14++0F29              ; MOV r16,imm16, invokes INT 20h, then resumes at HLT after handler IRET.
  15++0F29              ; The test checks all MOV destinations, interrupt return, FLAGS and SP.
  16++0F29              ; Success: status=0, AX=1A02h. Failures: status=9/11 with stage-specific AX codes.
  17++0F29              ; =========================================================================
  18++0F29
  19++0F29              X86_REAL_STEP_LIMIT EQU #FFFF
  20++0F29
  21++0F29              x86_real_mode_test:
  22++0F29 AF                   xor     a
  23++0F2A 32 C5 16             ld      (x86_real_bounds_enable), a
  24++0F2D CD 89 03             call    x86_mem_init
  25++0F30
  26++0F30                      ; Copy main guest program to physical 0000:5002.
  27++0F30 21 CB 16             ld      hl, x86_guest_main_image
  28++0F33 11 02 50             ld      de, #5002
  29++0F36 01 7C 00             ld      bc, x86_guest_main_image_end - x86_guest_main_image
  30++0F39 CD 6F 10             call    x86_real_copy_image
  31++0F3C DA 88 16             jp      c, x86_real_test_fail_1
  32++0F3F
  33++0F3F                      ; Copy interrupt handler to physical 0000:5100.

 474++12AB 77                   ld      (hl), a
 475++12AC 23                   inc     hl
 476++12AD 3A AE 16             ld      a, (x86_real_stack_value+1)
 477++12B0 77                   ld      (hl), a
 478++12B1 C3 04 11             jp      x86_real_next
 479++12B4
 480++12B4              ; IN: DE=word. On failure SP is rolled back; guest FLAGS are untouched.
 481++12B4              x86_real_stack_push:

 497++12DE 37                   scf
 498++12DF C9                   ret
 499++12E0
 500++12E0              ; OUT: x86_real_stack_value = popped word. SP advances only after read.
 501++12E0              ; POP SP's destination write then replaces the incremented SP as required.
 502++12E0              x86_real_stack_pop:

 512++12F9 23                   inc     hl
 513++12FA 22 76 09             ld      (x86_reg_sp), hl
 514++12FD B7                   or      a
 515++12FE C9                   ret
 516++12FF
 517++12FF              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 518++12FF              ; Short relative control flow: 70-7F Jcc, E0-E3 LOOP/JCXZ, EB JMP.
 519++12FF              ; Displacements are signed 8-bit values added to IP AFTER the displacement
 520++12FF              ; byte has been fetched. All target fetches still pass through the bounded
 521++12FF              ; CS:IP fetch path; guest FLAGS are never modified by these instructions.
 522++12FF              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 523++12FF
 524++12FF              x86_real_jmp_short:
 525++12FF CD AD 10             call    x86_real_fetch8
 526++1302 D8                   ret     c
 527++1303 32 BB 16             ld      (x86_real_imm_byte), a
 528++1306 C3 09 13             jp      x86_real_apply_rel8
 529++1309

 532++130C 5F                   ld      e, a

 534++130F CB 7F                bit     7, a
 535++1311 28 01                jr      z, .positive
 536++1313 15                   dec     d

 541++131B C3 04 11             jp      x86_real_next
 542++131E
 543++131E              ; E0 LOOPNZ, E1 LOOPZ, E2 LOOP, E3 JCXZ.
 544++131E              x86_real_loop_short:

 554++132F                      ; LOOP family decrements CX without changing any guest FLAGS bit.

 577++1358 FE E0                cp      #E0

 586++1368 C3 04 11             jp      x86_real_next

 694++1456              x86_real_jcc_jl:

 724++1493 CB 77                bit     6, a                  ; ZF
 725++1495 C2 DA 14             jp      nz, x86_real_jcc_taken
 726++1498                      ; Otherwise JL condition (SF != OF).
 727++1498 3A 88 09             ld      a, (x86_reg_flags)

 739++14B5

 741++14B5 3A 88 09             ld      a, (x86_reg_flags)
 742++14B8 CB 77                bit     6, a                  ; ZF must be clear
 743++14BA C2 E0 14             jp      nz, x86_real_jcc_not_taken
 744++14BD                      ; Otherwise JGE condition (SF == OF).
 745++14BD 3A 88 09             ld      a, (x86_reg_flags)
 746++14C0 CB 7F                bit     7, a
 747++14C2 28 0B                jr      z, .sf_zero
 748++14C4 3A 89 09             ld      a, (x86_reg_flags+1)
 749++14C7 CB 5F                bit     3, a
 750++14C9 C2 DA 14             jp      nz, x86_real_jcc_taken
 751++14CC C3 E0 14             jp      x86_real_jcc_not_taken
 752++14CF              .sf_zero:

 754++14D2 CB 5F                bit     3, a
 755++14D4 CA DA 14             jp      z, x86_real_jcc_taken
 756++14D7 C3 E0 14             jp      x86_real_jcc_not_taken
 757++14DA
 758++14DA              x86_real_jcc_taken:
 759++14DA 3A BB 16             ld      a, (x86_real_imm_byte)
 760++14DD C3 09 13             jp      x86_real_apply_rel8
 761++14E0              x86_real_jcc_not_taken:
 762++14E0 C3 04 11             jp      x86_real_next
 763++14E3
 764++14E3              ; 40-47 INC r16 and 48-4F DEC r16.
 765++14E3              ; The register file is contiguous in 8086 encoding order (AX,CX,DX,BX,
 766++14E3              ; SP,BP,SI,DI). INC/DEC preserve CF and all non-status/control FLAGS bits.
 767++14E3              x86_real_inc_r16:
 768++14E3 32 BA 16             ld      (x86_real_opcode), a
 769++14E6 AF                   xor     a
 770++14E7 32 BC 16             ld      (x86_real_incdec_mode), a
 771++14EA C3 F5 14             jp      x86_real_incdec_r16
 772++14ED
 773++14ED              x86_real_dec_r16:
 774++14ED 32 BA 16             ld      (x86_real_opcode), a
 775++14F0 3E 01                ld      a, 1
 776++14F2 32 BC 16             ld      (x86_real_incdec_mode), a
 777++14F5
 778++14F5              x86_real_incdec_r16:
 779++14F5 3A BA 16             ld      a, (x86_real_opcode)
 780++14F8 E6 07                and     7
 781++14FA 87                   add     a, a

 783++14FC 16 00                ld      d, 0
 784++14FE 21 6E 09             ld      hl, x86_reg_ax
 785++1501 19                   add     hl, de
 786++1502 22 C1 16             ld      (x86_real_reg_ptr), hl
 787++1505
 788++1505                      ; Save original operand before changing the selected guest register.
 789++1505 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
 790++1506 32 BD 16             ld      (x86_real_old_low), a
 791++1509 23                   inc     hl
 792++150A 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
 793++150B 32 BE 16             ld      (x86_real_old_high), a
 794++150E
 795++150E 3A BD 16             ld      a, (x86_real_old_low)
 796++1511 6F                   ld      l, a
 797++1512 3A BE 16             ld      a, (x86_real_old_high)
 798++1515 67                   ld      h, a
 799++1516 3A BC 16             ld      a, (x86_real_incdec_mode)
 800++1519 B7                   or      a
 801++151A 20 03                jr      nz, .decrement_word
 802++151C 23                   inc     hl
 803++151D 18 01                jr      .store_word
 804++151F              .decrement_word:
 805++151F 2B                   dec     hl
 806++1520              .store_word:
 807++1520 22 C3 16             ld      (x86_real_result), hl
 808++1523 ED 5B C1 16          ld      de, (x86_real_reg_ptr)

 810++1528 12                   ld      (de), a
 811++1529 13                   inc     de

 813++152B 12                   ld      (de), a
 814++152C
 815++152C CD 32 15             call    x86_real_update_incdec_flags
 816++152F C3 04 11             jp      x86_real_next
 817++1532
 818++1532              ; Update only OF/SF/ZF/AF/PF. CF is copied from the old FLAGS word;
 819++1532              ; all reserved/control bits are retained. PF is even parity of result AL.
 820++1532              x86_real_update_incdec_flags:
 821++1532 3A 88 09             ld      a, (x86_reg_flags)
 822++1535 E6 01                and     1
 823++1537 32 BF 16             ld      (x86_real_new_flags), a
 824++153A AF                   xor     a
 825++153B 32 C0 16             ld      (x86_real_new_flags+1), a
 826++153E
 827++153E                      ; PF: even parity of the low result byte.
 828++153E 2A C3 16             ld      hl, (x86_real_result)
 829++1541 7D                   ld      a, l
 830++1542 A7                   and     a
 831++1543 EA 48 15             jp      pe, .set_pf
 832++1546 18 05                jr      .no_pf
 833++1548              .set_pf:
 834++1548 21 BF 16             ld      hl, x86_real_new_flags
 835++154B CB D6                set     2, (hl)
 836++154D              .no_pf:
 837++154D                      ; ZF: full 16-bit result is zero.
 838++154D 2A C3 16             ld      hl, (x86_real_result)
 839++1550 7C                   ld      a, h
 840++1551 B5                   or      l
 841++1552 20 05                jr      nz, .no_zf
 842++1554 21 BF 16             ld      hl, x86_real_new_flags
 843++1557 CB F6                set     6, (hl)
 844++1559              .no_zf:
 845++1559                      ; SF: result bit 15.
 846++1559 2A C3 16             ld      hl, (x86_real_result)
 847++155C CB 7C                bit     7, h
 848++155E 28 05                jr      z, .no_sf
 849++1560 21 BF 16             ld      hl, x86_real_new_flags
 850++1563 CB FE                set     7, (hl)
 851++1565              .no_sf:
 852++1565                      ; AF: INC when old low nibble was F; DEC when it was 0.
 853++1565 3A BD 16             ld      a, (x86_real_old_low)
 854++1568 E6 0F                and     #0F
 855++156A 47                   ld      b, a
 856++156B 3A BC 16             ld      a, (x86_real_incdec_mode)
 857++156E B7                   or      a
 858++156F 20 07                jr      nz, .check_dec_af

 870++1581              .no_af:

 873++1584 FE FF                cp      #FF
 874++1586 20 15                jr      nz, .no_of
 875++1588 3A BE 16             ld      a, (x86_real_old_high)
 876++158B FE 7F                cp      #7F

 888++15A0 B7                   or      a

   1++2E1A              ; =========================================================================
   2++2E1A              ; PROJECT 124 — BIOS INT 10h text services, Stage 3A
   3++2E1A              ;
   4++2E1A              ; Project text-console profile: 42 columns x 12 rows, 6x8 glyphs.
   5++2E1A              ; Implemented functions:
   6++2E1A              ;   AH=00h set mode 03h (project 42x12 text profile)
   7++2E1A              ;   AH=0Fh get mode (AL=03h, AH=42, BH=0)
   8++2E1A              ;   AH=02h set cursor (BH=0, DH=row, DL=column)
   9++2E1A              ;   AH=03h get cursor (DH=row, DL=column)
  10++2E1A              ;   AH=0Eh teletype output (AL=character, BH=0)
  11++2E1A              ;
  12++2E1A              ; INT 10h enters through the normal IVT/stack frame. The BIOS service itself
  13++2E1A              ; is a firmware trap, then returns through the normal IRET primitive.
  14++2E1A              ; Host stream protocol over DBIT/GSDAT:
  15++2E1A              ;   nonzero byte = teletype character
  16++2E1A              ;   00 00        = clear the lower debug console
  17++2E1A              ;   00 01 row col= set lower debug console cursor
  18++2E1A              ; =========================================================================
  19++2E1A
  20++2E1A              BIOS_VIDEO_COLUMNS EQU 42
  21++2E1A              BIOS_VIDEO_ROWS    EQU 12
  22++2E1A              BIOS_VIDEO_MODE    EQU #03
  23++2E1A

  29++2E23 FE 0F                cp      #0F

  35++2E32 FE 0E                cp      #0E
  36++2E34 CA 23 2F             jp      z, bios_int10_teletype
  37++2E37 B7                   or      a
  38++2E38 C9                   ret

# file opened: bootstrap.asm
   1+ 4000              ; =========================================================================
   2+ 4000              ; PROJECT 124 — first-stage NeoGS executable bootstrap
   3+ 4000              ;
   4+ 4000              ; GS105a command #14 (RECEIVE BLOCK) loads this raw Z80 image into fixed RAM
   5+ 4000              ; at $4000. GS105a command #13 (JUMP TO ADDRESS) enters it at $4000.
   6+ 4000              ;
   7+ 4000              ; The bootstrap maps RAM over the low ROM window, receives a 16-bit firmware
   8+ 4000              ; length and exactly that many bytes through the documented ZX data channel,
   9+ 4000              ; stores the custom firmware at RAM address $0000, then jumps to $0000.
  10+ 4000              ;
  11+ 4000              ; It executes from the fixed RAM window $4000-$7FFF and keeps its stack below
  12+ 4000              ; $4000. The fixed $4000-$7FFF RAM window remains visible after B_NOROM is set.
  13+ 4000              ; =========================================================================
  14+ 4000
  15+ 4000              neogs_bootstrap_start:
  16+ 4000 F3                   di
  17+ 4001 31 FF 3F             ld      sp, #3FFF
  18+ 4004
  19+ 4004                      ; Hide the stock ROM and expose RAM at logical $0000-$3FFF.
  20+ 4004 DB 0F                in      a, (GSCFG0)
  21+ 4006 E6 FD                and     #FD                    ; clear RAMRO: low RAM must be writable
  22+ 4008 F6 01                or      M_NOROM                ; map RAM over the stock ROM at $0000
  23+ 400A D3 0F                out     (GSCFG0), a
  24+ 400C
  25+ 400C                      ; Tell Spectrum that the tiny loader is running and waiting for length.
  26+ 400C 3E A1                ld      a, NGS_BOOTSTRAP_READY_ACK
  27+ 400E CD 8B 40             call    bootstrap_send_data_byte
  28+ 4011
  29+ 4011                      ; Host sends length low byte, then high byte.
  30+ 4011 CD 77 40             call    bootstrap_receive_data_byte
  31+ 4014 5F                   ld      e, a
  32+ 4015 CD 77 40             call    bootstrap_receive_data_byte
  33+ 4018 57                   ld      d, a
  34+ 4019
  35+ 4019                      ; Reject zero length and any image larger than the reserved low RAM area.
  36+ 4019 7A                   ld      a, d
  37+ 401A B3                   or      e
  38+ 401B 28 22                jr      z, bootstrap_error
  39+ 401D 7A                   ld      a, d
  40+ 401E FE 30                cp      #30
  41+ 4020 38 06                jr      c, bootstrap_length_ok
  42+ 4022 20 1B                jr      nz, bootstrap_error
  43+ 4024 7B                   ld      a, e
  44+ 4025 B7                   or      a
  45+ 4026 20 17                jr      nz, bootstrap_error
  46+ 4028
  47+ 4028              bootstrap_length_ok:
  48+ 4028                      ; Explicitly acknowledge that the length is accepted before the host
  49+ 4028                      ; starts streaming the firmware payload.
  50+ 4028 3E A3                ld      a, NGS_BOOTSTRAP_LENGTH_ACK
  51+ 402A CD 8B 40             call    bootstrap_send_data_byte
  52+ 402D 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
  53+ 4030
  54+ 4030              bootstrap_copy_loop:
  55+ 4030 7A                   ld      a, d
  56+ 4031 B3                   or      e
  57+ 4032 28 08                jr      z, bootstrap_start_firmware
  58+ 4034 CD 77 40             call    bootstrap_receive_data_byte
  59+ 4037 77                   ld      (hl), a
  60+ 4038 23                   inc     hl
  61+ 4039 1B                   dec     de
  62+ 403A 18 F4                jr      bootstrap_copy_loop
  63+ 403C
  64+ 403C              bootstrap_start_firmware:
  65+ 403C C3 00 00             jp      0
  66+ 403F
  67+ 403F              bootstrap_error:
  68+ 403F 3E E1                ld      a, NGS_BOOTSTRAP_ERROR_ACK
  69+ 4041 CD 8B 40             call    bootstrap_send_data_byte
  70+ 4044 C3 47 40             jp      bootstrap_halt
  71+ 4047
  72+ 4047              ; Terminal state used when the host stops responding or cannot consume data.
  73+ 4047              ; Never leave a bootstrap polling loop running forever with no timeout.
  74+ 4047              bootstrap_halt:
  75+ 4047 F3                   di
  76+ 4048              .bootstrap_halt_loop:
  77+ 4048 76                   halt
  78+ 4049 18 FD                jr      .bootstrap_halt_loop
  79+ 404B
  80+ 404B              ; Best-effort error ACK after a receive timeout. This path has its own
  81+ 404B              ; bounded waits and never calls bootstrap_send_data_byte recursively.
  82+ 404B              bootstrap_timeout:
  83+ 404B 3E E1                ld      a, NGS_BOOTSTRAP_ERROR_ACK
  84+ 404D 32 B6 40             ld      (bootstrap_tx_byte), a
  85+ 4050 01 FF FF             ld      bc, #FFFF
  86+ 4053              .timeout_wait_slot:
  87+ 4053 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
  88+ 4055 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
  89+ 4057 28 08                jr      z, .timeout_slot_ready
  90+ 4059 0B                   dec     bc
  91+ 405A 78                   ld      a, b
  92+ 405B B1                   or      c
  93+ 405C 20 F5                jr      nz, .timeout_wait_slot
  94+ 405E C3 47 40             jp      bootstrap_halt
  95+ 4061              .timeout_slot_ready:
  96+ 4061 3A B6 40             ld      a, (bootstrap_tx_byte)
  97+ 4064 D3 03                out     (ZXDATWR), a
  98+ 4066 01 FF FF             ld      bc, #FFFF
  99+ 4069              .timeout_wait_host:
 100+ 4069 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 101+ 406B CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
 102+ 406D 28 D8                jr      z, bootstrap_halt
 103+ 406F 0B                   dec     bc
 104+ 4070 78                   ld      a, b
 105+ 4071 B1                   or      c
 106+ 4072 20 F5                jr      nz, .timeout_wait_host
 107+ 4074 C3 47 40             jp      bootstrap_halt
 108+ 4077
 109+ 4077              ; Wait for one byte from Spectrum. Reading ZXDATRD clears DBIT.
 110+ 4077              bootstrap_receive_data_byte:
 111+ 4077 01 FF FF             ld      bc, #FFFF
 112+ 407A              .rx_wait:
 113+ 407A DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 114+ 407C CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a

 121+ 4088              .rx_ready:

 123+ 408A C9                   ret

 126+ 408B              bootstrap_send_data_byte:

 128+ 408E 01 FF FF             ld      bc, #FFFF
 129+ 4091              .tx_free:

 132+ 4095 28 08                jr      z, .tx_slot_free

 137+ 409C C3 47 40             jp      bootstrap_halt
 138+ 409F              .tx_slot_free:

 140+ 40A2 D3 03                out     (ZXDATWR), a
 141+ 40A4 01 FF FF             ld      bc, #FFFF
 142+ 40A7              .tx_wait_read:
